# Desktop worktree cleanup — 2026-11-05

Completed the proof-first inventory; deleted **0** worktrees and reclaimed **0 bytes**. Every inventoried path is HELD. Existing worktree branches and tags were preserved. No stash, protected directory, service, runner, Docker volume, or qualification directory was changed. Only this task branch receives the inventory report commit.

Operator cleanup authority was already explicit; no additional go-ahead was requested. Automatic DesktopLegacyReleasePolicyService release was neither invoked nor enabled. No builds or tests ran.

Free-space movement reflects this live machine, including concurrent qualification and evidence writes; it is not attributed to cleanup. Sizes are logical file bytes, not allocated clusters, and omit reparse targets. Protected paths outside the worktree root were counted by name/registration only. The preserved special root directories (.antiphon, .retained, renamed remnants, and land) received metadata-only size measurements; registered child worktrees are separate rows. A root/child pair overlaps, so the sum is not unique or reclaimable bytes. Rows are an observation over time, not an atomic snapshot.

## Why no deletion passed

Proof 6 explicitly requires no local branches or tags ahead of origin. After a successful `git fetch origin`, the shared Antiphon repository had 250 such refs covering 885 unique commits. This was applied as the brief’s global gate to that shared repository. The complete ref/SHA list is below; none were pushed, deleted, relabelled, or otherwise repaired. Even a clean canonical worktree whose HEAD is published remains held under this gate. A later operator can decide whether publication/custody recovery or a narrower explicitly authorized rule is appropriate. Foreign-repository worktrees have separate P2/P6 custody/origin-audit holds; Antiphon refs are not used as evidence about their publication.

Proof keys: P1 terminal status/48h age or fixture/24h age; P2 protected/live/Blocked/standing owner or noncanonical registration; P3 status/error/root Git identity; P4 index flags or unprovable flag probe; P5 case-sensitive status or probe error; P6 origin containment/unpublished refs or unaudited foreign origin; P7 sequencer or unavailable administrative directory; P8 nested Git/submodule or unprovable stage probe; P9 ignored secret-shaped or non-build files or probe error; P10 longest absolute entry >=240 or incomplete filesystem traversal. A held/excluded row may stop before remaining proofs; unexecuted proofs are not asserted to pass.

Task states: Blocked=2, Canceled=16, Dispatched=10, Failed=9, Succeeded=669. Terminal age is based on server completedAt at the inventory-start cutoff, never directory age. The initial task list is a start-of-pass snapshot; supplements and concurrent arrivals have their own observation times. Standing-agent working directories were compared against the inventory, and all nonterminal task worktree/working directories from the scoped list were held.

## Surprises and operator decisions

- The five `antiphon-landgraph-canary-<32 lowercase hex>` directories match the producer in `tools/Antiphon.LandGraphAudit/CanaryRepository.cs` (DirectoryPrefix and sibling source-copy creation) and contain Antiphon.sln. They are older than 24h, but are ordinary copied source trees without root .git or a canonical Git worktree registration. All five have a longest absolute path of 244 characters. The only authorized remover, `git worktree remove`, cannot be established for them; no recursive-delete fallback was attempted. Fixture origin is inferred from name/producer/layout, not treated as deletion authority.

- Unregistered task remnants, renamed `.removing-*` directories, retained evidence, `.antiphon`, land worktrees, and registered worktrees outside the task-worktree root are preserved. An independent registration/custody decision is needed for these.

- Several directories unregistered with canonical Antiphon Git actually belong to other local repositories (their administrative .git paths identify the owners, including markdown-package and torquay-leander). They are not assumed to be orphaned. Their origins were not fetched/audited, and they remain held for their repository owners.

- `card-task-a7b2b64a-rc` and `card-task-c490e425` have .git links naming absent canonical administrative directories; Git probes exit 128. Their P4/P7 holds mean unprovable flags/sequencer state, not detected hidden index flags or active Git operations. Successful probes observed no non-H flags or active sequencer state. `card-task-c490e425` also contains six nested .git directories under ignored acceptance evidence; all were preserved.

- Fourteen measured paths fail the 240-character limit: the five canary roots (244), six renamed .removing roots (271), .retained (261), card-task-a7b2b64a-rc (262), and card-task-c490e425 (328). No filesystem traversal errors occurred in these measurements.

- The live b19a9880 qualification identifier did not resolve as an agent task (404), and did not match an inventoried path. No qualification/release/nightly directory was traversed, and no service/runner was restarted.

- The initial project query excluded 128 unscoped tasks. Fourteen initially unmatched standard task-directory IDs were fetched individually and resolved terminal. The nonstandard `card-task-a7b2b64a-rc` directory is associated by ID prefix for context only; its registration proof remains decisive.

- Cross-checked all 7109 distinct local worktree coordinates returned by the server (including individual supplements): 702 existed and 6407 were absent. All existing coordinates were under C:\Antiphon\worktrees; no additional outside directory needed traversal. Absent historical task coordinates are retained in external server-paths.json rather than expanded into thousands of nonexistent directory rows.

- Python 3.10 rejected some .NET timestamps with 4/5 fractional digits during the first pass. Those rows were re-probed using normalized fractional precision; final inventory has no UNKNOWN rows.

## Final verification and drift

No `git worktree remove` command ran; no deletion batch occurred. Final Git registration list was re-read, and concurrent arrivals were explicitly preserved. There is no deletion result to infer from disk-space or registration drift.

New paths observed after the initial inventory are HELD by P1/P2 because no deletion proofs were attempted for concurrent arrivals: `C:\Antiphon\worktrees\card-task-374d7fa0`, `C:\Antiphon\worktrees\card-task-9e4c7963`, `C:\Antiphon\worktrees\card-task-ff3ef62e`.

Paths absent from the final census: none. No removal by this task was attempted.

Prune dry-run and actual prune transcripts are in `final-state.json`; prune never used --expire=now or --force and only Git’s ordinary missing-directory metadata pruning was permitted. Final prune dry-run was empty; ordinary `git worktree prune --verbose` exited 0. It reported no removals.

## Reproducible evidence

- `inventory.py`: read-only initial census/probes; local inherited Git children only, two workers, GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0.

- `tasks.json`, `missing-tasks.json`, `standing-agents.json`: selected ownership/state metadata, without tokens or report bodies.

- `server-paths.json`, `supplementary-sizes.json`: complete server coordinate existence census and metadata-only sizes of preserved special directories.

- `worktrees-before.json`, `inventory.json`, `inventory-reconciled.json`, `rows/`: observations and repaired full inventory.

- `unpublished-local-refs.json`: preserved ref/SHA list after fetch.

- `final-state.json`: final registrations, directory drift, disk free, prune results.
